# Agarimoo
Categories: Sea Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs
Your Chumcap Bucket trembles, it's an Agarimoo.
[Agarimoo]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 81
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 55,000; [[Damage]]: 500
[Fishing Info]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing XV (15) ([[Fishing]])
Other Requirements: [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]]
[Drops]
Drops: 35–55x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 3x [[Agarimoo Tongue]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]]
XP: +1,012 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 106
[Metadata]
ID: AGARIMOO_35
An Agarimoo is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]].
## Spawning
An Agarimoo has a 15% (1 in 6.67 sea creatures) base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns in water near [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]] that the player placed themselves with [[Fishing|Fishing XV (15)]] or higher. This roll happens after the roll for hotspot sea creatures. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|Your Chumcap Bucket trembles, it's an Agarimoo.|
|Getting near an Agarimoo|The Agarimoo's fumes are damaging you!|

|-|-|-|-|

|Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|60%|
|Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|15|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Agarimoo Tongue]]|3|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Aquamarine Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/2.5M|

|[[Coins]]|101|101|101|
|Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|1,012|1,012|1,012|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|106|106|106|
### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.

|-|-|-|-|
|Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|15|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

# Alligator
Aliases: Aligator
Categories: Sea Creatures, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs
A long snout breaks the surface of the water. It's an Alligator!
[Alligator]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 132
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Animal]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 600,000; [[Damage]]: 600
[Fishing Info]
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 100
Fishing Location: [[Backwater Bayou]]
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing XX (20) ([[Fishing]])
Effective Bait: [[Whale Bait]] [[Light Bait]] [[Dark Bait]]
[Drops]
Drops: 2–4x [[Enchanted Lily Pad]] 1x [[Alligator Skin]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]] 0–1x [[Condensed Lily Pad]]
XP: +2,640 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 332
[Metadata]
ID: ALLIGATOR_120
Alligator is a LEGENDARY [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water in the [[Backwater Bayou]].
## Spawning
Alligator can be fished up from water in the [[Backwater Bayou]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XX (20)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Alligator is 100, resulting in a default catch chance of 1% (1 in 100 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A long snout breaks the surface of the water. It's an Alligator!|
## Behavior
Alligators chase after their target, rolling occasionally. They can also throw the player into the air. When throwing the player, the following dialogue appears in chat:
You were thrown by the Alligator!
The Alligator may occasionally cling onto the player in a death roll, dealing immense damage. During this, the following dialogue appears in chat:
The Alligator has you in a DEATH ROLL!
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv132]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Lily Pad]]|2-4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Alligator Skin]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Condensed Lily Pad]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
|[[Aquamarine Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/50k|
[Non-item drops - [Lv132]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|264|264|264|
|Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|2,640|2,640|2,640|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|332|332|332|
Alligator can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] to obtain [[Alligator Shard]] and +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

## Trivia
- During [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha]] testing, the Alligator used the model of a swimming player.
  - This was changed due to issues with rendering it on Minecraft 1.8.9, which still had to be supported at the time.

An Atoll Croaker is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
An Atoll Croaker can be fished up anywhere on the [[Lotus Atoll]] once the player is [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Atoll Croaker is 5,000, resulting in a default catch chance of 50% (1 in 2 sea creatures) with all [[Sea Creatures]] unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase its weight:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Common Hook]]|1.25x|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|An inquisitive Atoll Croaker takes the bait!|
## Behavior
An Atoll Croaker walks towards the player to attack them with its tongue.

A Blue Ringed Octopus is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] in water.
## Spawning
A Blue Ringed Octopus can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] in water. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXV (35)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Blue Ringed Octopus is 100, resulting in a catch chance of 1.00% (1 in 100 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A garish set of tentacles arise. It's a Blue Ringed Octopus!|

[Metadata]
ID: CARROT_KING_25
Is that even a fish? It's the Carrot King!
The Carrot King is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that has a chance to be caught in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] while using [[Carrot Bait]].
## Spawning
Carrot King has a 5% (1 in 20 sea creatures) base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns while using [[Carrot Bait]] in water with [[Fishing|Fishing XIV (14)]] or higher. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|Is that even a fish? It's the Carrot King!|
## Behavior
The Carrot King jumps around, attacking the player and dealing 275 [[Damage]] and [[Knockback|True Knockback]].

The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Trivia
- Although the Carrot King is an animal, it is unable to be brought to [[Jake]] on the Desert Island as it despawns above a certain Y level.
- The [[Advanced Rabbit Axe|Rabbit Axe]] can multiply all common stackable drops by 3.
  - It doesn't work on corrupted Carrot King.
- It's Bestiary entry is a reference to Monty Python and the Holy Grail. Namely, the "Rabbit of Caerbannog" encountered in the film.
  - It also uses the unused "Killer Bunny" model in Vanilla Minecraft , which shares the same reference.

ID: DROWNED_CAPTAIN_74
A Drowned Captain is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
A Drowned Captain can be fished up anywhere on the [[Lotus Atoll]] once the player is [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Drowned Captain is 500, resulting in a default catch chance of 5% (1 in 20 sea creatures) with all [[Sea Creatures]] unlocked.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Drowned Captain takes hold of your bobber!|
## Behavior
A Drowned Captain moves towards the player to attack them. It summons 5 tridents to throw that respawn after 2 seconds.

## Spawning
Dumpster Diver can be fished up from water in the [[Backwater Bayou]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing V (5)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Dumpster Diver is 2,875, resulting in a default catch chance of 28.75% (1 in 3.48 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.

Ent is an EPIC [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Ent can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level XII (12)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Ent is 500, resulting in a default catch chance of 5% (1 in 20 sea creatures) with all [[Sea Creatures]] unlocked. Currently, there are no modifiers that can be used to increase its weight.

Fiery Scuttler is a LEGENDARY [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished from [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Fiery Scuttler can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] on the [[Crimson Isle]] after reaching [[Fishing|Fishing XXXVII (37)]].
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Fiery Scuttler is 100, resulting in a catch chance of 1.00% (1 in 100 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|

## Spawning
The Fire Eel can be fished up from any Lava source in the [[Crimson Isle]] with a [[Fishing Rods|Lava Fishing Rod]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXIV (34)]] to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Fire Eel is 280, resulting in a default catch chance of 6.02% (1 in 16.6 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:

## Spawning
Fireproof Witch can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXII (22)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:

The Fireproof Witch moves toward players and throws splash Damage Potions. It also ignites nearby players.

ID: FLAMING_WORM_50
The Flaming Worm is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from lava while in the [[Precursor Remnants]].
## Spawning
A Flaming Worm can be fished up from any lava source in the [[Precursor Remnants]] with a Lava [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] if the player has [[Fishing|Fishing XIX (19)]]. The Flaming Worm can only be fished up if the lava source is above Y level 64.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Flaming Worm is 180, resulting in a default catch chance of 100.00% (1 in 1 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
(Note that this weight modification will have no effect in practice, as it is the only Sea Creature that can be caught in this pool.)
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Flaming Worm surfaces from the depths!|

ID: FROG_PRINCE_132
Frog Prince is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
Frog Prince can be fished up anywhere on the [[Lotus Atoll]] once the player is [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Frog Prince is 25, resulting in a default catch chance of 0.25% (1 in 400 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|Bow down before the Frog Prince... or pay the hefty price!|
## Behavior
When caught, the Frog Prince hops towards the player while shooting projectiles. The projectiles slowly move towards the player and can turn to follow them. Upon reaching the player, they explode into a ring of fire, dealing damage. Every 3 seconds, the Frog Prince hops 8 blocks into the air and smashes down, also dealing damage.

It's a snowman! He looks harmless.
Frosty is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Spawning
Frosty can be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing I (1)]] to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Frosty is 2875, resulting in a default catch chance of 28.75% (1 in 3.48 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there's no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|It's a snowman! He looks harmless.|
## Behavior
3 seconds after being caught up, it will throw snowballs at all players in a 32 block radius.

## Strategy
It can be difficult to kill Frosty due to the [[Knockback]] and high fire rate from its weapon. A [[Slime Hat]], [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]] or [[Shellwise Shard]] is recommended to prevent [[Knockback]]. The recommended way to kill it is to use a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] or [[Swords]] due to [[Sea Creatures]] being immune to arrows from most bows.

# Giant Isopod
Aliases: Isopod
Categories: Sea Creatures, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing
A Giant Isopod was dredged up from the depths!
[Giant Isopod]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 186

[Fishing Info]

Fishing Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]

Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing XV (15) ([[Fishing]])
[Drops]
Drops: 16–32x [[Enchanted Helix Log]] 8x [[Enchanted Ruby Veilshroom]] 0–1x [[Helixis]] 0–1x [[Veilshroom Bunch]] 0–1x [[Reinforced Netting]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]] 0–1x [[Isopod Husk]]
XP: +4,650 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 934
[Metadata]
ID: GIANT_ISOPOD_186
Giant Isopod is a MYTHIC [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water exclusively on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
Giant Isopod can be fished up from water in [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level XV (15)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Giant Isopod is 25, resulting in a default catch chance of 0.25% (1 in 400 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Giant Isopod was dredged up from the depths!|
## Behavior
When caught, the Giant Isopod spawns atop the [[Torrhus Springs]], regardless of its catch location. It will indiscriminately attack players in its vicinity. Upon being damaged to half [[Health]], the Giant Isopod travels down the [[Torrhus Springs]] all the way to the [[Desert Temple]] at the bottom, similarly to the [[Nessie]], and begins to fight players again once it has arrived. It is completely invulnerable during its journey down.

Giant Isopod can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] to obtain [[Giant Isopod Shard]] and +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

A gorF is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
A gorF can be fished up anywhere on the [[Lotus Atoll]] once the player is [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for a gorF is 1,500, resulting in a default catch chance of 15% (1 in 6.67 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|What even is that?! A... gorF?|
## Behavior
A gorF walks towards the player to attack them with its tongue. Occasionally, it teleports a few blocks around.

Grim Reaper is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up during, one hour before, and one hour after the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Spawning
Grim Reaper can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]], while the [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the [[Village]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXVI (26)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Spooky Hook V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|+15%|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Grim Reaper is 25, resulting in a catch chance of 0.25% (1 in 400 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|This can't be! The manifestation of death himself!|
## Behavior
The Grim Reaper shoots projectiles at the player, similar to the [[Frozen Scythe]]'s ability.

The Grinch stole Jerry's gifts... Get them back!
The Grinch is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can only be fished up in [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Spawning
Grinch can be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XIII (13)]] to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Grinch is 1500, resulting in a default catch chance of 15% (1 in 6.67 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|The Grinch stole Jerry's gifts... Get them back!|
## Behavior
It runs very fast, and often falls into the void while escaping the player that caught it up. It takes only 1 [[Health]] of damage each hit and drops 1 [[Gifts|Gift]] every time it is damaged.

## Strategy
Since this [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] is [[Timid]], it is recommended to use the [[Stealth]] enchantment in melee or to kill it using [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]], [[Frosty the Snow Blaster]] and [[Frosty the Howitzer]] at a distance.
## Trivia
A Runic Grinch
- The name of the sea creature is a reference to Dr. Seuss's book How The Grinch Stole Christmas.
- In popular culture, Grinch has become the likes of an anti-Santa character that steals gifts from people and prevents Christmas from coming to homes.
- When killed, the Grinch gives the player the [[Achievements|Achievement]] "Caught the Grinch". It grants 15 achievement points.
- Similarly to [[Hidden Jerry|Hidden Jerrys]], they run away from the player, sometimes falling into the void.
- Before the 0.24.3 update Grinch had unique health bar, which resulted in situations where if it was runic or corrupted, health bar would be much larger than normal.
- Strangely, part of the text that appears when fished up can be clicked on, leading to the invalid URL "https://Gifts...get". This is likely a bug.

An Inkling is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] in water.
## Spawning
An Inkling can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] in water. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing VII (7)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Inkling is 2,875, resulting in a catch chance of 28.75% (1 in 3.48 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Squid Hat]]|2x|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You get an inkling that you've caught... an Inkling!|
## Behavior
An Inkling throws Glow Ink Sacs at the player every second. If hit, it applies [[Blindness Potion|Blindness]] for 2 seconds.

Jumpin' Jack is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Spawning
Jumpin' Jack can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]], while the [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the [[Village]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing VI (6)]] to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Spooky Hook V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|+15%|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Jumpin' Jack is 5,000, resulting in a catch chance of 50% (1 in 2 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Common Hook]]|1.25x|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|Watch out! It's Jumpin' Jack.|
## Behavior
The Jumpin' Jack jumps around like a slime. If it gets too close to the player, it explodes into pumpkin particles.

ID: LAVA_BLAZE_100
A Lava Blaze is an EPIC [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from lava while in the [[Magma Fields]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XX (20)]] to be caught.
## Spawning
A Lava Blaze can be caught from any lava source in the [[Magma Fields]] with a Lava [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] if the player has [[Fishing|Fishing XX (20)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Lava Blaze is 36, resulting in a default catch chance of 50.00% (1 in 2 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
(Note that this weight modification will have no effect in practice, as it affects both of the Sea Creatures that can be caught in this pool.)
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Lava Blaze has surfaced from the depths!|
## Behavior
The Lava Blaze attacks players by shooting fireballs rapidly, similarly to normal [[Blaze|Blazes]], except dealing much higher damage.

# Lava Pigman
Categories: Sea Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs
A Lava Pigman arose from the depths!
[Lava Pigman]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])

[Drops]

XP: +500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 194
[Metadata]
ID: LAVA_PIGMAN_100
The Lava Pigman is an EPIC [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be [[Fishing|fished]] up from lava while in the [[Magma Fields]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXII (22)]] to be fished up, and has 450,000 [[Health]] .
## Spawning
A Lava Pigman can be fished up from any lava source in the [[Magma Fields]] with a Lava [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] if the player has [[Fishing|Fishing XXII (22)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Lava Pigman is 36, resulting in a default catch chance of 50.00% (1 in 2 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
(Note that this weight modification will have no effect in practice, since it affects both Sea Creatures that can be caught in this pool.)
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Lava Pigman has surfaced from the depths!|
## Behavior
The Lava Pigman mainly attacks players by shooting fireballs, dealing massive amounts of damage. It will also hit the players directly if nearby. However, the player has to hit the Lava Pigman first for it to attack. It is unknown if this is a bug or not. It takes no [[Knockback]] when hit.

# Lord Jawbus
Aliases: Jawbus
Categories: Sea Creatures, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs, Crimson Isle
You have angered a legendary creature... Lord Jawbus has arrived.
[Lord Jawbus]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 597
Mob Type: [[Magmatic]] [[Construct]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 100,000,000; [[Damage]]: 10,000
[Fishing Info]

Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 8
Fishing Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Rod Type: Lava ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing XLV (45) ([[Fishing]])
Effective Bait: [[Whale Bait]] [[Light Bait]] [[Dark Bait]]
Trophy Hunter Requirement ([[Odger]]): Adept Trophy Fisher
[Drops]
Drops: 16–32x [[Silver Magmafish]] 1x [[Magma Lord Fragment]] 0–1x [[Radioactive Vial]] 0–1x [[Bobbin' Scriptures]] 0–1x [[Carmine Dye]] 0–1x [[Lord Jawbus Shard]]
XP: +14,925 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 2985
[Metadata]
ID: LORD_JAWBUS_600
Lord Jawbus is a MYTHIC [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from lava on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Lord Jawbus can be fished up from any Lava source on the [[Crimson Isle]]. They require [[Fishing|Fishing XLV (45)]] or higher to be fished up, as well as [[Odger|Adept Trophy Fisher]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Lord Jawbus is 8, resulting in a default catch chance of 0.17% (1 in 581 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You have angered a legendary creature... Lord Jawbus has arrived.|
## Behavior
Once spawned, Lord Jawbus walks around shooting fireballs for 2,500 [[Damage]] and a laser for 30,000 [[Damage]] at nearby players.
After it drops to 60m, 40m or 20m [[Health]], Lord Jawbus will float upwards from its current position, spawning 2 Jawbus Followers which are any non-[[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] [[Sea Creatures]] fished on the [[Crimson Isle]] except for [[Plhlegblast]], [[Thunder]], or Lord Jawbus itself. Then, Lord Jawbus will fire TNT upwards and towards players which explode when hitting the ground. Once the Jawbus Followers are killed, the fire sphere around Lord Jawbus will dissipate and it will return to attacking players.

|[[Coins]]|2,985|2,985|2,985|
|Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|14,925|14,925|14,925|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|2,985|2,985|2,985|
Lord Jawbus can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Lord Jawbus Shard]] and +1,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+10,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

## Tips
- If a player plans to draw Lord Jawbus' aggro or does not want to die for [[Loot Sharing|loot share]], [[Bronze Hunter Armor]], [[Silver Hunter Armor]], [[Gold Hunter Armor]] or [[Diamond Hunter Armor]] can make them immune to damage.
  - This also makes Lord Jawbus immune to the player's damage.
## Trivia
- Despite the message when Lord Jawbus is fished up, they are [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] and not [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
- Killing Lord Jawbus without any assistance unlocks the "Jawbus Destroyer" task in [[Pandora's Box]].

A Lotus Guardian is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
A Lotus Guardian can be fished up anywhere on the [[Lotus Atoll]] once the player is [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for a Lotus Guardian is 2,875, resulting in a default catch chance of 28.75% (1 in 3.48 sea creatures) with all [[Sea Creatures]] unlocked.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Lotus Guardian emerges, ready to protect the Atoll.|
## Behavior
A Lotus Guardian walks towards the player to attack. Every 2 seconds, the Lotus Guardian stomps the ground and deals damage to all players in a 5 block radius.

The Magma Slug is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
The Magma Slug can be fished up from any body of Lava in the [[Crimson Isle]] with a Lava [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXVII (27)]] to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Magma Slug is 1,600, resulting in a default catch chance of 34.42% (1 in 2.91 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|From beneath the lava appears a Magma Slug.|
## Behavior
When killed, a Magma Slug splits into 3 [[Baby Magma Slug|Baby Magma Slugs]]. Only the final Baby Magma Slug to be killed will drop loot.

The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Spawning
Mithril Grubbers can be fished up in [[Abandoned Quarry]] located within the [[Dwarven Mines]] island. They require [[Fishing|Fishing level VI (6)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] to fish up Mithril Grubbers is 700 in total, but varies depending on the type.

# Nessie
Categories: Sea Creatures, More info needed, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Fishing, Mobs
You've caused a disturbance in the loch. Could it be... Nessie?
[Nessie]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 292
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Elusive]] [[Animal]]

Effective Bait: [[Whale Bait]] [[Light Bait]] [[Dark Bait]]
[Drops]
Drops: 16–32x [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] 2–4x [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]] 0–1x [[Mound of Seagrass]] 0–1x [[Vibrant Coral]] 0–1x [[Bobbin' Scriptures]] 0–1x [[Mangcore]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]]
XP: +7,300 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1463
[Metadata]
ID: NESSIE_302
Nessie is a MYTHIC [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Nessie can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level XXV (25)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Nessie is 25, resulting in a default catch chance of 0.25% (1 in 400 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase its weight:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You've caused a disturbance in the loch. Could it be... Nessie?|
## Behavior
When caught, Nessie spawns in the [[Murkwater Loch]] regardless of catch location. It deals damage in a [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) block area around it. Upon reaching 1m [[Health]], it shrinks, becomes invulnerable, and swims into the [[Murkwater Depths]]. It either swims to the [[Driptoad Delve]] or the [[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)|Dragon's Lair]]. Once it reaches its destination, it will rapidly enlarge until it can once again be damaged, also attempting to attack the player once it has fully enlarged.

Nessie can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] to obtain [[Nessie Shard]] and +10,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.
### Loot Sharing
Loot Sharing with Nessies treats all drops obtained by the player who kills the Nessie as 100% drops, causing the same drops to be given to all players participating in Loot Sharing.

## Trivia
- Nessie is based on the Loch Ness Monster from Scottish folklore, which is also commonly named Nessie.
- Attacking Nessie with a [[Soul Whip]] or [[Flaming Flay]] during its invulnerability phase counts as ghost damage towards [[Loot Sharing|Loot Share]] requirements.
## Bugs
- [[Nessie Shard]] is not a guaranteed drop even if the killer has an active [[Salts|Salt]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- If one player drops an item from Nessie, all players eligible for [[Loot Sharing|Loot Share]] receive it as well.[Confirm]

Nightmare is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Spawning
Nightmare can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]], while the [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the [[Village]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XIV (14)]] to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Spooky Hook V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|+15%|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Nightmare is 1,500, resulting in a catch chance of 15.00% (1 in 6.67 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You hear trotting from beneath the waves, you caught a Nightmare.|
## Behavior
Its special ability tosses the player up in the air and deals damage. The [[Slime Hat]] does not affect this attack.

Nutcracker is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Spawning
Nutcracker can be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XVIII (18)]] to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Nutcracker is 500, resulting in a default catch chance of 5% (1 in 20 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You found a forgotten Nutcracker laying beneath the ice.|
## Behavior
The Nutcracker quickly goes towards the player to attack while in water, and very slowly if not. If the Nutcracker isn't killed quickly, it will summon 500,000 [[Health]] [Lv25] [[Nutcracker|Toy Soldiers]].
When a Toy Soldier spawns, the following message appears in chat:
CRACK! A Toy Soldier has come to protect the Nutcracker!

# Oasis Rabbit
Categories: Sea Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Fishing
An Oasis Rabbit appears from the water.
[Oasis Rabbit]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 4
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 6,000
[Fishing Info]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 300
Fishing Location: [[Oasis]]
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing X (10) ([[Fishing]])
Effective Bait: [[Whale Bait]] [[Light Bait]] [[Dark Bait]]
[Drops]
Drops: 0–15x [[Enchanted Raw Rabbit]] 1–2x [[Lily Pad]] 1x [[Raw Rabbit]] 0–1x [[Rabbit's Foot]] 0–1x [[Rabbit Hide]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]]
XP: +40 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 5
[Metadata]
ID: OASIS_RABBIT_10
The Oasis Rabbit is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that spawns while fishing in the [[Oasis]] after reaching [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]]. They act like normal [[Rabbit|Rabbits]], but will shoot the player with arrows if it has not been damaged in a while.
Bringing one to [[Jake]] will allow the player to purchase the [[Lucky Dice]].
## Spawning
An Oasis Rabbit can be fished up from water in the [[Oasis]] with a water [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] if the player has [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Oasis Rabbit is 300, resulting in a default catch chance of 5.66% (1 in 17.65 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|An Oasis Rabbit appears from the water.|

The Oasis Sheep is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that spawns while fishing in the [[Oasis]] after unlocking [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]]. They act like normal [[Sheep]], and deal no damage to the player.
Bringing one to [[Jake]] will allow the player to purchase the [[Hardened Wood]].
## Spawning
An Oasis Sheep can be fished up from water in the [[Oasis]] with a water [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] if the player has [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Oasis Sheep is 700, resulting in a default catch chance of 13.22% (1 in 7.57 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there's no modifiers that can be used to increase it.

# Phantom Fisher
Categories: Sea Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Events, Spooky Festival, Fishing, Mobs
The spirit of a long lost Phantom Fisher has come to haunt you.
[Phantom Fisher]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 157
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Spooky]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000; [[Damage]]: 1,000
[Fishing Info]
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 100
Fishing Location: Any Water Source
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing XXI (21) ([[Fishing]])
Effective Bait: [[Spooky Bait]] [[Whale Bait]] [[Light Bait]] [[Dark Bait]]
Event Required: [[Spooky Festival]]
[Drops]
Drops: 2–4x [[Purple Candy]] 1–2x [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] 0–1x [[Spooky Hook]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]] 0–1x [[Deep Sea Orb]] 0–1x [[Midnight Dye]]
XP: +3,140 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 393
[Metadata]
ID: PHANTOM_FISHERMAN_160
Phantom Fisher is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Spawning
Phantom Fisher can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]], while the [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the [[Village]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXI (21)]] to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Spooky Hook V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|+15%|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Phantom Fisher is 100, resulting in a catch chance of 1.00% (1 in 100 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|The spirit of a long lost Phantom Fisher has come to haunt you.|
## Behavior
It constantly strafes and attacks using the [[Phantom Rod]], similar to the strafe pattern of a [[Crypt Souleater]].

# Plhlegblast
Aliases: Phlegblast
Categories: Sea Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs, Crimson Isle

[Plhlegblast]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])

[Fishing Info]

[Drops]
Drops: 30–50x [[Ink Sac]] 2–3x [[Enchanted Lily Pad]] 1x [[Lily Pad]] 0–1x [[Bobbin' Scriptures]] 0–1x [[Carmine Dye]] 0–1x [[Squid Boots]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Ink Sac]]
XP: +5,250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1053
[Metadata]
ID: POND_SQUID_300
A Plhlegblast is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from lava in the [[Plhlegblast Pool]].
## Spawning
A Plhlegblast has a 0.1% (1 in 1,000 sea creatures) base chance to override other [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] spawns while fishing in the [[Plhlegblast Pool]] with [[Fishing|Fishing XXXVI (36)]] or higher. Certain modifiers can be used to increase its chance:
|Condition|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Squid Hat]]|2x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|WOAH! A Plhlegblast appeared.|
## Behavior
The Plhlegblast does not attack the player, and is passive, similarly to the [[Squid]] and [[Inkling]]. It takes Lava damage, making it easy to kill. It has 500M [[Health]].
## Usage
Bringing a Plhlegblast to [[Kuudra Believer]] grants the player an option to obtain [[Herring the Fish]] and [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Helmet]] under specific conditions.

## Tips
Because a Plhlegblast can suffocate in the air easily, it is recommended to splash a [[Water Breathing Potion|Water Breathing VI]] potion to make it immune to suffocation if used for the purpose of the Kuudra Follower quest.
## Trivia
- The Plhlegblast has the highest [[Health]] of any [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] in SkyBlock.
- While the Plhlegblast is fished from lava, it does not count toward the [[Reforging|Chomp]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] bonus.

## Spawning

# Puddle Jumper
Categories: Sea Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs

[Puddle Jumper]

[Fishing Info]

XP: +25 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 2
[Metadata]
ID: PUDDLE_JUMPER_85
Puddle Jumper is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Spawning
Puddle Jumper can be fished up anywhere on the [[Lotus Atoll]] once the player is [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Puddle Jumper is 100, resulting in a default catch chance of 1% (1 in 100 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Puddle Jumper Hook]]|5x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Puddle Jumper is preparing for liftoff—cast your rod into it and hold on tight!|
## Behavior
When caught, a red halo appears around the Puddle Jumper. It waits for the player to cast their fishing line onto it. After 5 seconds, regardless of whether the player has hooked it, the halo turns green and the Puddle Jumper hops to another location. A new red halo appears first upon landing and shows COME AND GET ME! on its head, acting as a timer before the next jump.
If the player successfully hooks the Puddle Jumper, they will be pulled along as it jumps. The Puddle Jumper will hop a total of 5 times before stopping and shows RIBBIT above every time they jump. It drops its loot 13 seconds after the final halo appears.
Additionally, if the player does not miss a single hook throughout the encounter, they are awarded with an extra [[Silver Lotus]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Hooking the jumper with a Fishing Rod|[MOB] Puddle Jumper: Let's get ready for a ride!|
|Trying to hit the jumper|[MOB] Puddle Jumper: Woah there! Throw ya hook onto me and let's go jumpin!|
|After hooking on the creature's last stop|[MOB] Puddle Jumper: Wow! You caught me! [MOB] Puddle Jumper: Great jumpin! Try to catch me every time!|
|After 5 successful hooks without missing once|[MOB] Puddle Jumper: Wow! You're a master jumper! Here's a bonus; on me! The Puddle Jumper awarded you with a Silver Lotus for making every jump!|

## Notes
- It's not necessary for any player to hook the Puddle Jumper, as it can finish its journey on its own.
- The player who fishes up a Puddle Jumper always gets its loot, even without jumping with it.
## Tips
- Donating the [[Trophy Frogs|Puddle Jumper Trophy Frog]] to [[Researcher Ribery]] grants significantly more [[Lotus]] than either its base drop or the bonus [[Silver Lotus]] for jumping with it 5 times.
  - The only case this is false is if the player drops a [[Gold Lotus]].
## Trivia
- Despite not being a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], the [[Grappling Hook]] can be used to hook the Puddle Jumper.
- Puddle Jumper doesn't get affected by [[Expertise]] [[Fishing]] XP boost.
- During the first and second testing sessions on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], fishing up a Puddle Jumper on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] would crash the lobby.
  - The ability to fish up Puddle Jumpers (as well as all other [[Lotus Atoll]] Sea Creatures) on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] was later disabled, presumably at least in part due to this bug.

## Spawning
The Pyroclastic Worm can be fished up from any Lava source on the Crimson Isle with a [[Fishing Rods|Lava Fishing Rod]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXI (31)]] to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Pyroclastic Worm is 400, resulting in a default catch chance of 8.61% (1 in 11.62 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.

# Ragnarok
Categories: Sea Creatures, Confirmations needed, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs

[Ragnarok]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 612

[Drops]
Drops: 16–32x [[Silver Magmafish]] 0–1x [[Brimstone Handle]] 0–1x [[Chain of the End Times]] 0–1x [[Burnt Texts]] 0–1x [[Bobbin' Scriptures]] 0–1x [[Carmine Dye]]
XP: +15,300 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 3061
[Metadata]
ID: RAGNAROK_666
Ragnarok is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished from [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Ragnarok can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XLVII (47)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Ragnarok is 25, resulting in a catch chance of 0.25% (1 in 400 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|The sky darkens and the air thickens. The end times are upon us: Ragnarok is here.|
## Behavior
The Ragnarok has two phases.
- Before reaching 50% [[Health]] , the Ragnarok will jump around the player 5 times in a row every 15s[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], rapidly shooting waves of fire and spirals of fire particles that deal immense damage.
- Upon reaching 50% [[Health]] , the Ragnarok becomes invulnerable for a period of time, summoning lightning strikes around the player.
- When under 50% [[Health]] , the Ragnarok will perform the same attacks, alongside converting blocks around it into Lime, Yellow, Orange, and Red Terracotta. Players within the area after reaching Red take significant damage.
If the Ragnarok is taken too far from it's original spawn point, about 40-50m, it will despawn. It will also despawn if pushed into the void.

# Reindrake

[Metadata]
ID: REINDRAKE_100
A Reindrake is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water in [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Spawning
A Reindrake can be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level XXXV (35)]] to be fished up. When a Reindrake is fished up, it sends a message to all players in chat.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Reindrake is 25, resulting in a default catch chance of 0.25% (1 in 400 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|Drake Piper I Perk ([[Essence Shops]])|1.1x|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Reindrake forms from the depths.|
|Announcement|WOAH! [player] summoned a Reindrake from the depths!|
|Announcement if [[Double Hook Chance]] procs|WOAH! [player] summoned TWO Reindrakes from the depths!|
|Killed|DEFEATED! A Reindrake was slain and dropped all its loot!|
## Behavior
A Reindrake will fly around in the air after being fished up. If players try to approach it, it will fly higher. It only takes 1 [[Health]] in damage every hit.
## Drops
A Reindrake has a 10% (1 in 10 hits) chance to drop [[Gifts]] when damaged. The player that spawned the Reindrake will receive a [[Gifts|Gift]] if another player who damages it gets one.
The chances to drop each Gift are below:
- [[White Gift]]: 65% (1 in 1.53)
- [[Green Gift]]: 25% (1 in 4)
- [[Red Gift]]: 10% (1 in 10)
While [[Foxy]] is Mayor or Minister with the A Time For Giving perk, the Reindrake can also drop [[Party Gift|Party Gifts]], with the following probabilities:
- [[White Gift]]: 61.94% (1 in 1.61)
- [[Green Gift]]: 23.809% (1 in 4.2)
- [[Red Gift]]: 9.523% (1 in 10.5)
- [[Party Gift]]: 4.761% (1 in 21)
The player who fished up the Reindrake can also drop following items:

|[[Iceberg Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/50k|
|[[Aquamarine Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/50k|

|[[Coins]]|930|930|930|
|Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|4,650|4,650|4,650|

## Tips
- Players that do not deal enough damage to a Reindrake may not earn [[Bestiary]] progress for it unless they fished it up.
  - [[Arrows|Explosive Arrows]] can help with dealing more damage.
  - Dealing at least one melee or magic damage hit to a Reindrake will guarantee Bestiary progress.
## Trivia
- The Reindrake is the only [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that triggers a server-wide announcement when caught.
- Despite being an [[Ender Dragon]], Reindrake does not have the [[Airborne]] mob type because there is a limit of three mob types per mob.
  - The [[Airborne]] mob type would also be essentially cosmetic alongside the [[Shielded]] mob type.
- The player who fished up the Reindrake will get on average 162.5 (2 stacks plus 34.5) [[White Gift]], 62.5 [[Green Gift]], and 25 [[Red Gift]], worth .

The Rider of the Deep has emerged.
The Rider of the Deep is an UNCOMMON [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from Water.
## Spawning
The Rider of the Deep can be caught from [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] upon reaching [[Fishing|Fishing XI (11)]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Rider of the Deep is 400, resulting in a default catch chance of 9.31% (1 in 10.74 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.

- Prior to version 0.9, the chicken could lay eggs that gave [[Farming]] XP like a normal chicken.
- Prior to a fix, the Fishing XP from killing the Monster of the Deep counted towards the XP required to spawn Revenants even though the message from starting a [[Zombie Slayer]] quest specifies that Combat XP is required.

Scarecrow is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Spawning
Scarecrow can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]], while the [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the [[Village]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing IX (9)]] to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Spooky Hook V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|+15%|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Scarecrow is 2,875, resulting in a catch chance of 28.75% (1 in 3.48 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|Phew! It's only a Scarecrow.|
## Behavior
In addition to melee attacks, it can throw rapidly spit out wheat as a ranged attack.

[Metadata]
ID: SEA_WALKER_4
You caught a Sea Walker.
A Sea Walker is a COMMON [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water.
## Spawning
A Sea Walker can be caught on the [[Hub Island]] and most other non-fishing islands. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing I (1)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for a Sea Walker is 800, resulting in a default catch chance of 18.62% (1 in 5.37 sea creatures) with all [[Sea Creatures]] unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Common Hook]]|1.25x|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You caught a Sea Walker.|
## Behavior
The Sea Walker will chase after their target like a normal [[Zombie]], but are a bit faster, attacking the player with a [[Diamond Sword]] and occasionally shooting a [[Bows|Bow]].

A Sea Witch is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] after reaching [[Fishing|Fishing VII (7)]].
## Spawning
A Sea Witch can be caught on the [[Hub Island]] and most other non-fishing islands. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing VII (7)]] or higher to be caught.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for a Sea Witch is 700, resulting in a default catch chance of 16.29% (1 in 6.14 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|It looks like you've disrupted the Sea Witch's brewing session. Watch out, she's furious!|
## Behavior
The Sea Witch will attempt to throw Splash Potion of Harming at the player at a highly accelerated rate compared to witches spawned on the player's island.

## Spawning
Silkbreeze can be fished up from water on [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level XV (15)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Silkbreeze is 100, resulting in a default catch chance of 1% (1 in 100 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:

A Snapping Turtle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] in water.
## Spawning
A Snapping Turtle can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] in water. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Snapping Turtle is 1,500, resulting in a catch chance of 15.00% (1 in 6.67 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Snapping Turtle is coming your way, and it's ANGRY!|
## Behavior
A Snapping Turtle approaches the player to hit them.

Sprawl can be fished up from water on [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level XV (15)]] or higher to be fished up.

Squid is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water.
## Spawning
Squid can be caught on the [[Hub Island]] and most other non-fishing islands. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing I (1)]] or higher to be fished up.
If the player is in the [[The Park]] where the rain bought from [[Vanessa]] is still active, there's a 66.6% chance to naturally spawn 1-2 Squids every 11 seconds when there are less than five squids present that were generated the same way.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Squid is 1,200, resulting in a default catch chance of 27.93% (1 in 3.58 sea creatures) with all [[Sea Creatures]] unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Common Hook]]|1.25x|
|[[Squid Hat]]|2x|

# Tadgang
Categories: Sea Creatures, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing
A gang of Liltads!
[Tadgang]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 29
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5,000; [[Damage]]: 100
[Fishing Info]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 1,500
Fishing Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing IX (9) ([[Fishing]])
[Drops]
Drops: 32–64x [[Mangrove Log]] 4–8x [[Sea Lumies]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] 0–1x [[Gill Membrane]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]]
XP: +362 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 37
[Metadata]
ID: TADGANG_FROG_8; TADGANG_FROG_10
A Tadgang is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
A Tadgang can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level IX (9)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for a Tadgang is 1,500, resulting in a default catch chance of 15% (1 in 6.67 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Currently, there are no modifiers that can be used to increase its weight.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A gang of Liltads!|
## Behavior
Tadgangs spawn in groups of 3 or 5 tadpoles when caught. They swim towards the player, despite being able to deal no damage. Each one has 1 [[Health]], even when [[Runecrafting|Runic]].
Once the final Tadgang is killed, it transforms into a frog with 5,000 [[Health]]. It walks towards the player to deal damage.

The Taurus is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from lava on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
The Taurus can be fished up from any Lava source on the [[Crimson Isle]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXV (35)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Taurus is 160, resulting in a default catch chance of 3.44% (1 in 29.05 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|

|-|-|

## Trivia
- Taurus bears some resemblance to the Hog Rider from Clash of Clans and Clash Royale.
- The word Taurus means "bull" in Latin and is also one of the twelve Zodiac signs.
